原词 prompt
15秒，横屏16:9。从图1参考两位演示者、实验台、瓶和托盘，保留冷紫色场景与人物外观。全片四个镜头，用硬切连接。镜头一中景两人准备，镜头二近景从蓝绿色瓶往托盘缓倒液体，镜头三俯拍搅拌直到液体乳白，镜头四人物抬头露出惊讶表情。在第4、8、12秒硬切，各镜头内保持连续。

<Subject 3> is the laboratory table and apparatus from <Picture 1>, comprising a dark tabletop, a clear rectangular container with a metal-rimmed tray insert, a teal plastic bottle with a red cap, frosted reagent bottles, and beakers.
<Subject 4> is the cold purple stage environment in <Picture 1>, characterized by horizontal stepped purple and dark platforms under cool studio illumination.

summary:
[reference generation] In a cold purple studio setting (<Subject 4>), two lab-coated demonstrators (<Subject 1> and <Subject 2>) prepare apparatus on a lab bench (<Subject 3>). <Subject 1> pours liquid from the teal bottle into the tray, stirs the mixture overhead until it turns milky white, and both demonstrators look up in surprise.

retention_analysis:

<Subject 2> (appears in [Shot 1], [Shot 4]): fully_preserved - retains the same female demonstrator's appearance, tied-back hair, safety goggles, white lab coat with green emblem, and protective gloves from <Picture 1>.
<Subject 3> (appears in [Shot 1], [Shot 2], [Shot 3]): fully_preserved - retains the bench layout, rectangular container with metal insert tray, teal bottle with red cap, and glassware from <Picture 1>.
<Subject 4> (appears in [Shot 1], [Shot 2], [Shot 4]): fully_preserved - retains the cool purple horizontal-tiered background lighting and stage ambiance from <Picture 1>.

detailed_description:

The camera remains static. Both demonstrators adjust their gloves and inspect the clear container and reagent bottles on the bench in preparation. Soft ambient room tone and subtle rustling of lab coats are audible.

[Shot 2] At 00:04.000, the shot cuts to a close-up of <Subject 1>'s gloved hands tilting the teal plastic bottle from <Subject 3> over the metal-rimmed tray container. Clear liquid streams smoothly from the bottle mouth into the basin. The camera holds steady on the fluid contact point. A steady liquid pouring sound echoes faintly against the quiet room ambiance.

[Shot 3] At 00:08.000, the shot cuts to a top-down overhead view looking directly into the clear container of <Subject 3>. A gloved hand inserts a glass stirring rod and swirls the liquid in circular motions. Swirls form as the transparent liquid turns into a cloudy, milky white suspension. The rhythmic clinking of glass against the container rim blends with gentle liquid swirling sounds.

[Shot 4] At 00:12.000, the shot cuts to a medium-close two-shot of <Subject 1> and <Subject 2> in front of <Subject 4>. Both demonstrators raise their heads from the workbench, their eyes widening behind their safety goggles into visible expressions of surprise and awe. The room ambiance remains quiet with faint settling murmurs.

<Subject 2> (appears in [Shot 1], [Shot 3], [Shot 4]): fully_preserved - The female demonstrator's ponytail, lab coat with green chest graphic, goggles, and gloves from <Picture 1> are maintained.
<Subject 3> (appears in [Shot 1], [Shot 2], [Shot 3], [Shot 4]): fully_preserved - The lab setup, teal chemical bottle, tray, glassware, and cool purple-striped stage lighting from <Picture 1> remain consistent.

[Shot 1] The camera remains in a static medium shot framing the entryway of <Subject 3>. At the opening, <Subject 2> begins closed within its wooden frame. She wears an ivory ribbed knit wool sweater over a subtle white collared shirt. Positioned in the center foreground, her hands hold the crisp edges of a textured cream paper letter just at the lower frame line. In the out-of-focus background, the soft silhouette of an upright piano, a wooden music stand holding blurred sheet music, and warm sunlit wooden shelves establish a quiet study room. Soft, natural daylight pours in from a window off-screen to the left, carving delicate highlights across her cheek and creating distinct glints in her irises. Her gaze begins cast downward, scanning the paper with focused intensity and slight furrowing of her eyebrows. Her pupils abruptly dart across the final line of the acceptance text; she takes a sharp, audible intake of breath through slightly parted lips as her shoulders rise. Immediately, her brows relax, the tension across her forehead dissipates, and a wide, radiant smile breaks across her face, crinkling the corners of her eyes with genuine delight. A glimmer of joyful tears wets her lower eyelids as she lets out a soft, breathy chuckle, tilting her chin down slightly in overwhelmed disbelief while holding the paper close.
overall_soundscape: A crisp, audible rustle of heavyweight paper handling is distinct in the foreground, immediately followed by a pronounced, sharp intake of breath through parted lips and a soft, quiet exhale of relief, set against the low, continuous room tone of a quiet domestic interior.
non_diegetic_music: A solo acoustic piano enters with a slow, delicate ascending melodic motif, underscored by warm, sustained cello notes that hold an even, gentle dynamic volume throughout.

判定点 搅拌动作的力度、速度都符合“轻轻缓慢”。

原词 prompt
8秒，横屏16:9。桌上有一杯满水。女子拿起金属勺搅拌，固定近景，杯沿与水面全程入画。轻轻缓慢地搅一圈，水面只起细纹。
PE 后 prompt
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, close-up with a static shot framing a transparent cylindrical glass cup filled almost to the brim with crystal-clear water, resting steadily on a matte, light-toned natural oak tabletop. The glass rim and the water's surface remain sharply focused and visible in frame. Soft morning daylight pours in from a window to the left, casting delicate, luminous caustics across the water and throwing faint reflections onto the wooden grain. To the right of the glass lies a slender, polished stainless steel spoon. An adult woman in her late twenties, wearing a soft cream knit sweater with ribbed cuffs, enters the frame from the right with her right hand, her fingers slender with clean natural nails. She smoothly lifts the spoon from the table, positioning it above the cup. With steady, deliberate composure, she dips the tip of the metal spoon just beneath the surface of the water near the inner rim. She gently and slowly glides the spoon in a single smooth circular path along the inner edge of the glass. The tranquil surface responds with subtle, fine concentric ripples and soft liquid crests that sweep toward the rim without spilling a single drop. Completing the single circle, she brings the spoon to a gentle rest in the center of the cup, her fingers holding it steady as the tiny water waves gradually settle back toward calm.
overall_soundscape: A distinct metallic scrape is audible as the spoon is picked up from the wooden tabletop, followed by a faint, soft liquid plunge as the metal tip enters the water. A gentle, clearly heard water swirl accompanies the circular motion, transitioning into a pronounced quiet room tone as the surface ripples smooth out.
non_diegetic_music: Minimalist acoustic score featuring a solo felt piano playing slow, sparse single notes in the middle register with long natural decay, underpinned by a sustained warm cello drone at a low, unchanging dynamic.

实际送入 H3 的文本
One uninterrupted 8-second vertical shot, camera bolted in place, keeping the SAME composition and scale from the first to the last frame. On <Picture 1>'s autumn old-town stone road, the little golden-furred monkey remains a small full-body figure in the bottom third. It walks slowly sideways a couple of steps in place on the path, maintaining its distance from camera. For the ENTIRE shot, feature four LARGE separate golden orange maple leaves with finely pointed crisp serrations hanging from a slender dark branch across the upper middle and center of the composition. Each of the two largest leaves is about ONE THIRD of the entire frame width, with its full outline visible against open clear pale blue sky. There are sky gaps between the leaves. The branch spans left to right, stays in shot, and its silhouette is legible. Frame the leaves and branch as the main subject; the monkey and traditional street below are secondary. Even at the last frame the leaves continue to dominate the same area. Daylight passes through the leaves to reveal fine veins. Leaves are tack sharp, contours clean and temporally stable. A very gentle breeze gives the whole leaf a slow continuous sway of only a few pixels. Locked focus on the large leaves; no focus pull, camera motion, flying leaves, changes in exposure or cuts. Outdoor ambience with faint rustling and paw steps.

原反例任务的 PE 文本（历史记录）
subject_definitions:
<Picture 1> is the first frame of [Shot 1], depicting a small furry monkey crouched on a stone road beneath an autumn tree canopy in an ancient town.
<Subject 1> is the young furry monkey character in <Picture 1>, featuring golden-brown fur, large luminous amber eyes, round ears, and a light-colored chest.

summary:
[keyframe completion] Starting from <Picture 1>, <Subject 1> crawls forward slowly along the sunlit stone path as the overhead autumn leaves sway in the breeze with subtle digital aliasing jitter along their backlit edges.

retention_analysis:
<Picture 1> ([Shot 1] first frame): fully_preserved - The composition, warm lighting, character placement, and ancient street environment match <Picture 1>.
<Subject 1> (appears in [Shot 1]): fully_preserved - Retains the golden fur, facial structure, amber eyes, and crouched posture from <Picture 1>, moving forward at a slow pace.

detailed_description:
The target video is in stylized 3D animation style with warm, soft cinematic lighting. [Shot 1] The shot begins from <Picture 1> as a Static Shot. In low-angle, eye-level framing, <Subject 1> is centered in the lower third of the vertical frame on the cobblestone path, looking toward the viewer before slowly crawling forward on all fours with measured steps. Overhead, golden autumn tree branches sway gently in a soft breeze. Along the backlit leaf edges at the top of the canopy, subtle digital aliasing micro-jitter flickers erratically across adjacent frames without a unified direction, while the leaf clusters maintain their natural swaying motion. In the background, pedestrians and rustic market stalls remain gently active under warm sunlight. non_diegetic_music:
N/A
校准反例制作方法
提取前景暖色叶片与亮天空的轮廓带。在中间两片大叶的下半部，对亮天空附近的轮廓带进行局部水平采样位移。不同叶齿小段各自保持1–4帧后换向，最大位移14个源像素；叶脉深处与其他区域保持源帧。叶面内部、人物与镜头沿用同一源帧，音轨逐包复制。

PE 后 prompt
integrated_multimodal_description: [Shot 1] Cinematic, frontal medium shot at eye level, the camera holds a static shot focused on a skilled adult potter in a rustic ceramics studio. The ceramicist, an adult in their mid-thirties with hair tied back in a neat bun, wears a charcoal-gray linen apron speckled with dried clay splatters over a short-sleeved earthy olive t-shirt, their bare forearms and hands thoroughly coated in wet gray clay slip. The potter sits centered behind a spinning electric pottery wheel, resting their elbows on their thighs while cupping both hands firmly and steadily around a wet clay mound, pressing their thumbs into the center while supporting the outer curved wall with glistening fingers. In the background, tall wooden open-slatted shelving units remain completely stationary, neatly stacked with unglazed bisque-fired vases, clay jugs, terra-cotta bowls, and assorted wooden sculpting tools. Soft, diffused daylight enters from a large off-frame workshop window to the side, balanced by a warm overhead pendant lamp that casts crisp specular highlights across the slick, glistening surface of the rotating clay bowl. As the wheel spins with steady rotational momentum, the potter gently exerts upward and outward pressure with their palms and fingertips, drawing the wet clay into a flared, perfectly symmetrical circular bowl. The potter slightly lowers their shoulders in intense focus, tilting their head down slightly while carefully easing finger pressure off the smoothed rim, leaving the round ceramic bowl rotating flawlessly on the wheel head.
overall_soundscape: A continuous low electric motor hum of the spinning pottery wheel is dominant in the foreground, accompanied by the distinct wet, squelching friction and sliding hiss of clay-coated palms smoothing wet slip against the spinning clay. Occasional soft water droplets drip audibly into the plastic splash pan alongside subtle fabric rustles from the potter's linen apron.
non_diegetic_music: Sparse acoustic nylon-string guitar playing gentle, slow-tempo arpeggiated chords with warm ambient reverb, maintaining a steady and tranquil rhythm without swells.
